- **godot-lsp**: GDScript code intelligence for Claude Code. Godot ships a real language server but only listens on TCP, while Claude Code runs every LSP server over stdio — its `transport: "socket"` option is accepted and then ignored, so the two never meet. This bridges them with a dependency-free 55-line pipe, giving hover, go-to-definition, find-references, symbols and diagnostics on .gd files from the engine's own analyser, built-in class documentation included.
